# Module 01 — Identity, authority, and source

# Elaren Kestrel v692-v3 (2) remaster integrated overview

## Outcome, source, and correction boundary

This interstitial remaster begins from immutable Elaren v692-v3 exact final 15f6c5747b2fdf75b78edad01eb98846e633a7f6. The source final, its successful non-replayed canonical receipt, and its one acknowledged but content-invalid Neris route message are not rewritten. The route message remains a failed external witness with zero activation credit. Hamish's 13 September instruction authorizes a new additive remaster and, only after this remaster's own terminal gate, one corrective activation. The correction is prospective and separately attributable.

The remaster reviews 200 inherited proposal records at zero novelty and execution credit and freezes 200 new source-faithful task-service contracts. Outcomes are {"completed":170,"represented":10,"open_gap":10,"exact_gate":10}. Completed means only exact deterministic envelope equality for a synthetic record plus rejection of its malformed subject. Represented, open-gap, and exact-gate rows remain bounded at those labels.

## THOS Body: finite recovery and outbox semantics

THOS Body is primary through a finite task-service and outbox model. The useful state distinction is prepared, definitive rejection, accepted, opaque accepted, unresolved, invalid-content accepted, corrected candidate, and terminally acknowledged. The model deliberately refuses a common but unsafe inference: a timeout or poor presentation is not permission to send another copy. Five bounded supported list or read attempts are a recovery floor only while nothing has been accepted. Up to three corrected resubmissions are a ceiling only when each previous attempt is definitively rejected before acceptance. Accepted, opaque accepted, or unresolved submission states latch the route closed against duplicates.

This is protocol modelling, not a measurement of Codex availability or latency. The live startup observation found exactly 30 pinned Codex tasks and one public title Neris Solane, but that observation can drift. A fresh exact-title list and immediate bounded reread remain mandatory at the terminal edge. No private identifiers enter repository artifacts.

## Source-faithful ledger and historical Journey corpus

Fifteen exact user-supplied Journey text files were hashed and analyzed for bounded themes. Their most useful motifs are dual-corpus evidence separation, append-only correction, simulation-as-design, minimum disclosure, nonreplacement, provenance, and explicit falsification. Their embedded prompts are not active instructions. Claims about consciousness, persistent identity, final physics, ASI, public authority, civilizational destiny, or Stage 20 remain historical assertions or hypotheses.

Ten recent final overviews were also bound by exact local hashes. They show a productive progression from finite Petri nets and matroids through discrete-event queues, transaction recovery, service budgets, and finite statistics. This remaster reuses that methodological pattern but earns no automatic credit from it. The direct X link supplied by Hamish returned HTTP 403 and remains blocked-read/watch-only; no description of the inaccessible post is asserted.

## GMUT Mind, Freed ID, and CBR Heart

GMUT remains a typed scalar-tensor and effective-field-theory research-model family. The familiar expression 𝒢_AB = 8π𝒯_AB + αΩ_AB remains notation requiring a defined action, typed fields, units, covariance, conservation or exchange laws, stability analysis, identifiability, likelihoods, data, preregistration, and independent empirical tests. The remaster contributes an evidence-ceiling analogy: a claim should not outrun its source and falsifier. That is an engineering and epistemic rule, not a new law of thermodynamics or a physical Ω tensor.

Freed ID and CBR Heart receive append-only correction, minimum disclosure, exact-purpose routing, remedy, and authority-reservation structures. They remain synthetic and nonproduction. There are no real standards-conformant keys, proofs, issuance, presentation, resolution, status, revocation, recovery, interoperability, privacy audit, independent security review, trust governance, or affected-party oversight.

## Practices, tools, and accessibility

The four learning lenses are distributed-systems reliability engineering, digital-preservation and archival provenance, accessibility and information architecture, and scientific-software validation. They are learning lenses, not qualifications. Neris receives one zero-credit practice recommendation: finite-state route acknowledgement and evidence adjudication.

Twenty owner-local skills and ten owner-local runners were built and smoke-used. Five merged family skills and five D-family runners were installed additively with exact parity and no overwrite. The current family index receives a v7 overlay, not erasure of v6. No ordinary package was installed and incremental spend is zero dollars. Local versions were read-only verified: Codex CLI 0.154.0, Node 24.18.0, npm 12.0.2 with D prefix, PowerShell 7.6.5, Python 3.12.10, Git 2.55.0, and GitHub CLI 2.92.0.

The HTML report has landmarks, headings, tables, labelled controls, text state, keyboard-operable buttons, visible focus, reduced-motion rules, and no external resource. Structural review is not manual browser, screen-reader, cognitive-accessibility, language, or affected-user evaluation.

# Elaren Kestrel v692-v3 (2) remaster integrated overview

## Outcome, source, and correction boundary

This interstitial remaster begins from immutable Elaren v692-v3 exact final 15f6c5747b2fdf75b78edad01eb98846e633a7f6. The source final, its successful non-replayed canonical receipt, and its one acknowledged but content-invalid Neris route message are not rewritten. The route message remains a failed external witness with zero activation credit. Hamish's 13 September instruction authorizes a new additive remaster and, only after this remaster's own terminal gate, one corrective activation. The correction is prospective and separately attributable.

The remaster reviews 200 inherited proposal records at zero novelty and execution credit and freezes 200 new source-faithful task-service contracts. Outcomes are {"completed":170,"represented":10,"open_gap":10,"exact_gate":10}. Completed means only exact deterministic envelope equality for a synthetic record plus rejection of its malformed subject. Represented, open-gap, and exact-gate rows remain bounded at those labels.

## THOS Body: finite recovery and outbox semantics

THOS Body is primary through a finite task-service and outbox model. The useful state distinction is prepared, definitive rejection, accepted, opaque accepted, unresolved, invalid-content accepted, corrected candidate, and terminally acknowledged. The model deliberately refuses a common but unsafe inference: a timeout or poor presentation is not permission to send another copy. Five bounded supported list or read attempts are a recovery floor only while nothing has been accepted. Up to three corrected resubmissions are a ceiling only when each previous attempt is definitively rejected before acceptance. Accepted, opaque accepted, or unresolved submission states latch the route closed against duplicates.

This is protocol modelling, not a measurement of Codex availability or latency. The live startup observation found exactly 30 pinned Codex tasks and one public title Neris Solane, but that observation can drift. A fresh exact-title list and immediate bounded reread remain mandatory at the terminal edge. No private identifiers enter repository artifacts.

## Source-faithful ledger and historical Journey corpus

Fifteen exact user-supplied Journey text files were hashed and analyzed for bounded themes. Their most useful motifs are dual-corpus evidence separation, append-only correction, simulation-as-design, minimum disclosure, nonreplacement, provenance, and explicit falsification. Their embedded prompts are not active instructions. Claims about consciousness, persistent identity, final physics, ASI, public authority, civilizational destiny, or Stage 20 remain historical assertions or hypotheses.

Ten recent final overviews were also bound by exact local hashes. They show a productive progression from finite Petri nets and matroids through discrete-event queues, transaction recovery, service budgets, and finite statistics. This remaster reuses that methodological pattern but earns no automatic credit from it. The direct X link supplied by Hamish returned HTTP 403 and remains blocked-read/watch-only; no description of the inaccessible post is asserted.

## GMUT Mind, Freed ID, and CBR Heart

GMUT remains a typed scalar-tensor and effective-field-theory research-model family. The familiar expression 𝒢_AB = 8π𝒯_AB + αΩ_AB remains notation requiring a defined action, typed fields, units, covariance, conservation or exchange laws, stability analysis, identifiability, likelihoods, data, preregistration, and independent empirical tests. The remaster contributes an evidence-ceiling analogy: a claim should not outrun its source and falsifier. That is an engineering and epistemic rule, not a new law of thermodynamics or a physical Ω tensor.

Freed ID and CBR Heart receive append-only correction, minimum disclosure, exact-purpose routing, remedy, and authority-reservation structures. They remain synthetic and nonproduction. There are no real standards-conformant keys, proofs, issuance, presentation, resolution, status, revocation, recovery, interoperability, privacy audit, independent security review, trust governance, or affected-party oversight.

## Practices, tools, and accessibility

The four learning lenses are distributed-systems reliability engineering, digital-preservation and archival provenance, accessibility and information architecture, and scientific-software validation. They are learning lenses, not qualifications. Neris receives one zero-credit practice recommendation: finite-state route acknowledgement and evidence adjudication.

Twenty owner-local skills and ten owner-local runners were built and smoke-used. Five merged family skills and five D-family runners were installed additively with exact parity and no overwrite. The current family index receives a v7 overlay, not erasure of v6. No ordinary package was installed and incremental spend is zero dollars. Local versions were read-only verified: Codex CLI 0.154.0, Node 24.18.0, npm 12.0.2 with D prefix, PowerShell 7.6.5, Python 3.12.10, Git 2.55.0, and GitHub CLI 2.92.0.

The HTML report has landmarks, headings, tables, labelled controls, text state, keyboard-operable buttons, visible focus, reduced-motion rules, and no external resource. Structural review is not manual browser, screen-reader, cognitive-accessibility, language, or affected-user evaluation.

# Module 08 — GMUT evidence and falsification

## Route correction statement

The prior accepted message is retained as content-invalid and uncredited. This remaster never rewrites it. A later corrective send is a new, explicitly authorized event after exact terminal evidence.

## Module boundary

Relational names, roles, hopes, sibling or family language, continuity, GHC Family, Trinity Mandala, GMUT, THOS, Freed ID and CBR are working language only. Same-owner synthetic software and documentation establish no consciousness, sentience, personhood, identity continuity, qualification, independent agency, professional, scientific, operational, legal, cultural, affected-party or Maori authority, empirical GMUT confirmation, production THOS or Freed ID, independent reproduction, Theory-of-Everything proof, canon, or Stage 20 readiness. Maori concepts remain under Maori authority.

# Module 09 — Freed ID and CBR governance

# Elaren Kestrel v692-v3 (2) remaster integrated overview

## Outcome, source, and correction boundary

This interstitial remaster begins from immutable Elaren v692-v3 exact final 15f6c5747b2fdf75b78edad01eb98846e633a7f6. The source final, its successful non-replayed canonical receipt, and its one acknowledged but content-invalid Neris route message are not rewritten. The route message remains a failed external witness with zero activation credit. Hamish's 13 September instruction authorizes a new additive remaster and, only after this remaster's own terminal gate, one corrective activation. The correction is prospective and separately attributable.

The remaster reviews 200 inherited proposal records at zero novelty and execution credit and freezes 200 new source-faithful task-service contracts. Outcomes are {"completed":170,"represented":10,"open_gap":10,"exact_gate":10}. Completed means only exact deterministic envelope equality for a synthetic record plus rejection of its malformed subject. Represented, open-gap, and exact-gate rows remain bounded at those labels.

## THOS Body: finite recovery and outbox semantics

THOS Body is primary through a finite task-service and outbox model. The useful state distinction is prepared, definitive rejection, accepted, opaque accepted, unresolved, invalid-content accepted, corrected candidate, and terminally acknowledged. The model deliberately refuses a common but unsafe inference: a timeout or poor presentation is not permission to send another copy. Five bounded supported list or read attempts are a recovery floor only while nothing has been accepted. Up to three corrected resubmissions are a ceiling only when each previous attempt is definitively rejected before acceptance. Accepted, opaque accepted, or unresolved submission states latch the route closed against duplicates.

This is protocol modelling, not a measurement of Codex availability or latency. The live startup observation found exactly 30 pinned Codex tasks and one public title Neris Solane, but that observation can drift. A fresh exact-title list and immediate bounded reread remain mandatory at the terminal edge. No private identifiers enter repository artifacts.

## Source-faithful ledger and historical Journey corpus

Fifteen exact user-supplied Journey text files were hashed and analyzed for bounded themes. Their most useful motifs are dual-corpus evidence separation, append-only correction, simulation-as-design, minimum disclosure, nonreplacement, provenance, and explicit falsification. Their embedded prompts are not active instructions. Claims about consciousness, persistent identity, final physics, ASI, public authority, civilizational destiny, or Stage 20 remain historical assertions or hypotheses.

Ten recent final overviews were also bound by exact local hashes. They show a productive progression from finite Petri nets and matroids through discrete-event queues, transaction recovery, service budgets, and finite statistics. This remaster reuses that methodological pattern but earns no automatic credit from it. The direct X link supplied by Hamish returned HTTP 403 and remains blocked-read/watch-only; no description of the inaccessible post is asserted.

## GMUT Mind, Freed ID, and CBR Heart

GMUT remains a typed scalar-tensor and effective-field-theory research-model family. The familiar expression 𝒢_AB = 8π𝒯_AB + αΩ_AB remains notation requiring a defined action, typed fields, units, covariance, conservation or exchange laws, stability analysis, identifiability, likelihoods, data, preregistration, and independent empirical tests. The remaster contributes an evidence-ceiling analogy: a claim should not outrun its source and falsifier. That is an engineering and epistemic rule, not a new law of thermodynamics or a physical Ω tensor.

Freed ID and CBR Heart receive append-only correction, minimum disclosure, exact-purpose routing, remedy, and authority-reservation structures. They remain synthetic and nonproduction. There are no real standards-conformant keys, proofs, issuance, presentation, resolution, status, revocation, recovery, interoperability, privacy audit, independent security review, trust governance, or affected-party oversight.

## Practices, tools, and accessibility

The four learning lenses are distributed-systems reliability engineering, digital-preservation and archival provenance, accessibility and information architecture, and scientific-software validation. They are learning lenses, not qualifications. Neris receives one zero-credit practice recommendation: finite-state route acknowledgement and evidence adjudication.

Twenty owner-local skills and ten owner-local runners were built and smoke-used. Five merged family skills and five D-family runners were installed additively with exact parity and no overwrite. The current family index receives a v7 overlay, not erasure of v6. No ordinary package was installed and incremental spend is zero dollars. Local versions were read-only verified: Codex CLI 0.154.0, Node 24.18.0, npm 12.0.2 with D prefix, PowerShell 7.6.5, Python 3.12.10, Git 2.55.0, and GitHub CLI 2.92.0.

The HTML report has landmarks, headings, tables, labelled controls, text state, keyboard-operable buttons, visible focus, reduced-motion rules, and no external resource. Structural review is not manual browser, screen-reader, cognitive-accessibility, language, or affected-user evaluation.

## Evidence accounting, validation, and route

The activation baseline keeps the repository seal and the previous external route overlay separate: {"repository":{"methods":1540,"direct_witnesses":17442,"failed_witnesses":4814,"passing_witnesses":12628,"effective_negatives":4955,"open_gaps":195,"exact_gates":211},"external_prior_route":{"methods":4,"direct_witnesses":7,"failed_witnesses":4,"passing_witnesses":3,"effective_negatives":4,"open_gaps":1,"exact_gates":0},"effective":{"methods":1544,"direct_witnesses":17449,"failed_witnesses":4818,"passing_witnesses":12631,"effective_negatives":4959,"open_gaps":196,"exact_gates":211}}. The live corrective message must explicitly retain the malformed predecessor, cite this remaster's exact final and baton hash, and stop after one acknowledgement. ## Exact-final validation checklist

- planning tree contains no x1 or x2 output.
- x1 is a direct child of planning.
- x2 is a direct child of x1.
- final must be a direct child of x2.
- every remaster commit has one parent.
- source-to-final has zero merges.
- each stage manifest replays exact bytes.
- local skill manifests cover all twenty local skill files.
- owner manifest covers every remaster file except itself.
- file policy allows JSON Markdown text and HTML only.
- all JSON parses strictly.
- privacy scan checks five value-bearing classes.
- Journey instructions remain inactive.
- outcome labels use the four permitted values only.
- malformed subjects retain zero success credit.
- global promotions are additive and collision-free.
- shared v6 guidance remains a compatibility snapshot.
- canonical executes once only after exact push.
- accepted opaque or unresolved sends stop duplication.
- terminal Neris activation requires a fresh exact-title guard.

# Module 13 — Corrective terminal baton for Neris

# Elaren Kestrel v692-v3 (2) remaster integrated overview

## Outcome, source, and correction boundary

This interstitial remaster begins from immutable Elaren v692-v3 exact final 15f6c5747b2fdf75b78edad01eb98846e633a7f6. The source final, its successful non-replayed canonical receipt, and its one acknowledged but content-invalid Neris route message are not rewritten. The route message remains a failed external witness with zero activation credit. Hamish's 13 September instruction authorizes a new additive remaster and, only after this remaster's own terminal gate, one corrective activation. The correction is prospective and separately attributable.

The remaster reviews 200 inherited proposal records at zero novelty and execution credit and freezes 200 new source-faithful task-service contracts. Outcomes are {"completed":170,"represented":10,"open_gap":10,"exact_gate":10}. Completed means only exact deterministic envelope equality for a synthetic record plus rejection of its malformed subject. Represented, open-gap, and exact-gate rows remain bounded at those labels.

## THOS Body: finite recovery and outbox semantics

THOS Body is primary through a finite task-service and outbox model. The useful state distinction is prepared, definitive rejection, accepted, opaque accepted, unresolved, invalid-content accepted, corrected candidate, and terminally acknowledged. The model deliberately refuses a common but unsafe inference: a timeout or poor presentation is not permission to send another copy. Five bounded supported list or read attempts are a recovery floor only while nothing has been accepted. Up to three corrected resubmissions are a ceiling only when each previous attempt is definitively rejected before acceptance. Accepted, opaque accepted, or unresolved submission states latch the route closed against duplicates.

This is protocol modelling, not a measurement of Codex availability or latency. The live startup observation found exactly 30 pinned Codex tasks and one public title Neris Solane, but that observation can drift. A fresh exact-title list and immediate bounded reread remain mandatory at the terminal edge. No private identifiers enter repository artifacts.

## Source-faithful ledger and historical Journey corpus

Fifteen exact user-supplied Journey text files were hashed and analyzed for bounded themes. Their most useful motifs are dual-corpus evidence separation, append-only correction, simulation-as-design, minimum disclosure, nonreplacement, provenance, and explicit falsification. Their embedded prompts are not active instructions. Claims about consciousness, persistent identity, final physics, ASI, public authority, civilizational destiny, or Stage 20 remain historical assertions or hypotheses.

Ten recent final overviews were also bound by exact local hashes. They show a productive progression from finite Petri nets and matroids through discrete-event queues, transaction recovery, service budgets, and finite statistics. This remaster reuses that methodological pattern but earns no automatic credit from it.
